15: lfsr_state_f\\n\");\n    sinkBitsFile.write(\" *      * 16: lfsr_out_f\\n\");\n    sinkBitsFile.write(\" */\\n\");\n\n    # Cycle after reset\n    sinkBitsFile.write(\"0_\" + bin16(0) + \"        // Cycle after reset, signals applied to load seed\\n\");\n    # Cycle the seed is loaded\n    sinkBitsFile.write(\"0_\" + bin16(0) + \"        // Seed is latched on this cycle, but monitor will \" + \\\n                       \"not see it on the output yet\\n\")\n    # Seed should have been loaded\n    lfsrOutMask = int(math.pow(2, 15))\n    lfsrOutFirst = (seed & lfsrOutMask) >> 15\n    sinkBitsFile.write(str(lfsrOutFirst) + \"_\" + bin16(seed) + \\\n                       \"        // Check seed of \" + str(seed) + \" got loaded\\n\")\n    # Setup masks\n    lfsrMask = int(math.pow(2, 16)) - i < ENTRIES; i = i + 1)\n                m_f[i] <= 0;\n        end\n        else\n        begin\n            wr_pointer_f        <= wr_pointer_next;\n            rd_pointer_f        <= rd_pointer_next;\n            entry_count_f       <= entry_count_next;\n            m_f[wr_pointer_f]   <= m_next;\n        end\n    end\n    \n    //\n    // Combinational logic\n    // \n\n    // Write and read pointers\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        wr_pointer_next = wr_pointer_f;\n        rd_pointer_next = rd_pointer_f;\n        if (wr_en)\n            wr_pointer_next = wr_pointer_f + 1;\n        if (rd_en && val)\n            rd_pointer_next = rd_pointer_f + 1;\n    end\n\n    // Write logic\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        m_next = m_f[wr_pointer_f];\n        if (wr_en)\n            m_next = din;\n    end\n\n    // Output data and valid signal\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        // Take care of case where we are writing and reading\n        // on the same cycle to the same location\n        if (rd_en && wr_en && rd_pointer_f == wr_pointer_f)\n        begin\n            dout = din;\n            val = 1'b1;\n        end\n        else\n        begin\n            dout = m_f[rd_pointer_f];\n            val = rd_en && ~empty;\n        end\n    end\n\n    // Entry count\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        entry_count_next = entry_count_f;\n        if (rd_en && !wr_en && (entry_count_f != 0))\n            entry_count_next = entry_count_f - 1;\n        else if (!rd_en && wr_en && (entry_count_f != ENTRIES))\n            entry_count_next = entry_count_f + 1;\n    end\n\n    // Full/empty logic\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        full = (entry_count_f == (ENTRIES-1));\n        empty = (entry_count_f == 0);\n    end\n\nendmodule\n","numLines":151,"startLine":1,"totalLines":151}}}
